## `@stackra/ui/react`

From `./src/react/index.ts`: 742 exported names and 5 re-exported from other packages.

| `Accordion` | Function | A stack of sections whose titles open and close their content. `expandedKeys` is the list of open ids; `onExpandedChange` is called with the new list whenever the user opens or closes a section. |
| `AccordionProps` | Type | Props of the web Accordion: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own accordion props, passed through unchanged. The sections are `items`, never children, and `canExpandMultiple` is HeroUI's `allowsMultipleExpanded`. |

| `AccountConnectionProps` | Type | Props of the web AccountConnection: the shared contract plus the Card's own props, passed through. The row is the component's, so `children` is refused. |
| `ActionMenu` | Function | A button that opens a menu of grouped actions, for the end of a row or the corner of a section. `label` names the button; the menu is named by it. |
| `ActionMenuProps` | Type | Props of the web ActionMenu: the shared contract plus the Menu's own pass-through props. The trigger and the selection belong to the component, so the type refuses them. |

| `AiMarkProps` | Type | Props of the web AiMark. |
| `Alert` | Function | An inline message: the tone's icon, the title, the description and the actions, announced by its tone. |
| `AlertDialog` | Function | A modal that interrupts the user to confirm or refuse one decision. Confirming calls `onConfirm` and closes it; every other way out (cancel, Escape, a press outside when `isDismissable`) calls `onCancel` and closes it. Pair `isOpen` with `onOpenChange` to control it, or leave both out and use `defaultOpen`. |
| `AlertDialogProps` | Type | Props of the web AlertDialog: the shared contract plus React Aria's dialog props, passed to the dialog element (`className`, `id`, `aria-describedby`, `data-*`). HeroUI's `role` is fixed at `alertdialog`, and its children are the contract's `title`, `description` and `children`, so the type refuses both. HeroUI's `status`, which is the contract's `tone`, is not a dialog prop, so the type refuses that as well. |
| `AlertProps` | Type | Props of the web Alert: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own alert props and the attributes of a `div`, passed through. HeroUI's `status` is the contract's `tone`, its `title` (the HTML tooltip) is the contract's `title`, and the role is the tone's, never the caller's. |

| `AngleSliderProps` | Type | Props of the web AngleSlider. |

| `FilterPanelProps` | Type | Props of the web FilterPanel: the shared contract plus the props of a `section`, passed through to the root. The panel draws its own content, so children are refused, and `label` owns the accessible name. |

Give it the form's `control` (or sit it inside `FormProvider`), the field's `name` and a `render` that spreads the binding first: `render={(binding) => <TextField {...binding} label="Email" />}`. Give the form `defaultValues`, so the field is controlled from its first render. |

Without a `label` it is decorative and hidden from assistive technology. The mirror is applied after the caller's `style`, so a caller's own `transform` cannot cancel it. |

Give it `selectionMode` to make it a list box whose rows are selected by press, Space and Enter; without one it is a plain list. `emptyState` shows only when nothing is loading and there are no items, and `loadingLabel` is spoken once for the spinner, not for every row. |
| `ListItem` | Function | A row of a list. Pressable when it has `onPress`, a button named by its title (or `label`); a disabled one is refused and dimmed by the button; a plain row is never dimmed. Every other `li` prop, `className` and `ref` included, reaches the `li`. |

| `MoneyValueProps` | Type | Props of the web MoneyValue. |

| `NativeSelectProps` | Type | Props of the web NativeSelect. |

The element's `aria-label` is the contract's `label` and its `title` attribute gives way to the contract's `title`, so the type refuses the first and replaces the second. |
| `NavigationList` | Function | The places of an app as a navigation landmark. Name it with `label` when the page holds more than one. The entry with `isCurrent` is announced as the current page. |

| `NavigationMenuProps` | Type | Props of the web NavigationMenu. |

| `PackageListProps` | Interface | Props of the web PackageList. |

The element's `aria-label` is the contract's `label` and its `title` attribute gives way to the contract's `title`, so the type refuses the first and replaces the second. |

| `PeriodPickerProps` | Type | Props of the web PeriodPicker. |

| `ProgressCircleProps` | Type | Props of the web ProgressCircle: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own progress circle props, passed through unchanged. HeroUI's `color` is the contract's `tone`, `isIndeterminate` follows from a missing `value`, `label` owns the accessible name, and the parts are drawn here, so `children` is refused; the value's number format is `valueLabel`, so `formatOptions` is too. |

| `PromptSuggestionsProps` | Type | Props of the web PromptSuggestions. |

| `SchedulerEvent` | Interface | One event on the schedule. |
| `SchedulerProps` | Type | Props of the web Scheduler. |
| `SchedulerView` | Constant | The views, smallest span first. |
| `SchedulingBoard` | Function | A scheduling board of resources over time. |
| `SchedulingBoardProps` | Type | Props of the web SchedulingBoard. |
| `Schematic` | Function | A machine of named parts joined by pipes. |
| `SchematicNode` | Function | One part of a schematic machine. |
| `SchematicNodeProps` | Interface | Props of the web SchematicNode. |
| `SchematicProps` | Interface | Props of the web Schematic. |
| `Screen` | Function | The container of one screen: a `main` that fills the window, scrolls when asked and keeps clear of the system's own parts on the edges named. Without `edges` it clears all of them. |
| `ScreenProps` | Type | Props of the web Screen: the shared contract plus the `main` element's own props, passed through. The element's `aria-label` is the contract's `label`, so the type refuses it. There is no `ref`: the root is a `main` or, when embedded, a `div`, and the two refs differ. |
| `ScrollArea` | Function | A region that scrolls with faded edges. |
| `ScrollAreaProps` | Type | Props of the web ScrollArea. |
| `SearchField` | Function | A search input with a clear button. The visible `label`, `description` and `errorMessage` are tied to the input for screen readers; while `isLoading` a decorative spinner replaces the search icon and the input is marked busy. |
| `SearchFieldProps` | Type | Props of the web SearchField: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own search field props, passed through unchanged. HeroUI's `fullWidth` is the contract's `isFullWidth`, its `placeholder` belongs to the input, and its children are composed here. |
| `Section` | Function | A titled card: title and description, actions and a body. Draw one per block of a detail page and give it a title the page does not repeat; two with the same title need a `label` each. |
| `SectionDock` | Function | A dock of the page's sections. |
| `SectionDockProps` | Interface | Props of the web SectionDock. |
| `SectionProps` | Type | Props of the web Section: the shared contract plus the card's own props, passed through. The element's `aria-label` is the contract's `label` and `aria-labelledby` is the title's, so the type refuses both; its `title` attribute gives way to the contract's `title`, and the element is a `section`, so `render` is not a prop here. |
| `SegmentedControl` | Function | A few exclusive choices with a sliding thumb (Day / Week / Month). |
| `SegmentedControlProps` | Type | Props of the web SegmentedControl: the shared contract plus React Aria's RadioGroup props, passed through. |
| `Select` | Function | A field that picks one key from a short list. The value is the chosen key, or `null` for nothing; `onChange` is called with the key as a string. A read-only field shows its value but never opens or changes, by pointer or keyboard. |
| `SelectProps` | Type | Props of the web Select: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own select props, passed through unchanged. HeroUI's `fullWidth` is the contract's `isFullWidth`; the app lists choices as `items`, never as children. |
| `Separator` | Function | A rule between two groups. Horizontal by default; a vertical one stretches to its row's height. Pass `variant` to match the surface it sits on: the rule does not read the level from the surface around it. |
| `SeparatorProps` | Type | Props of the web Separator: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own separator props, passed through unchanged. |
| `SettingRow` | Function | One setting: its words, its switch or action, and the detail that opens while it is on. |
| `SettingRowProps` | Type | Props of the web SettingRow: the shared contract plus the props of its outer `div`; the row draws its children. |
| `SettingsCell` | Function | One setting. `control` may be a function of the title and description ids. |
| `SettingsCellProps` | Type | Props of the web SettingsCell. |
| `SettingsColorCell` | Function | A setting that is a colour. |
| `SettingsColorCellProps` | Type | Props of the web SettingsColorCell. |
| `SettingsSelectCell` | Function | A setting with one choice from a list. |
| `SettingsSelectCellProps` | Type | Props of the web SettingsSelectCell. |
| `SettingsSliderCell` | Function | A setting on a scale. |
| `SettingsSliderCellProps` | Type | Props of the web SettingsSliderCell. |
| `SettingsSwitchCell` | Function | A setting turned on and off. |
| `SettingsSwitchCellProps` | Type | Props of the web SettingsSwitchCell. |
| `SetupGuide` | Function | An onboarding checklist with its progress, folding away once every task is done. |
| `SetupGuideProps` | Type | Props of the web SetupGuide: the shared contract plus the house Card's root props; the guide draws the children. |
| `ShareButton` | Function | Shares a link through the system sheet, or copies it where there is none. |
| `ShareButtonProps` | Type | Props of the web ShareButton. |
| `Sheet` | Constant | A sheet: a swipeable panel from a screen edge, by default the bottom, over a backdrop that blocks the page until it closes. Open it from a `trigger`, or drive it with `isOpen` and `onOpenChange`; give it `snapPoints` to rest part way; fill it with `Sheet.Header` (holding `Sheet.Title`), `Sheet.Body`, `Sheet.Footer` and `Sheet.Close`. A Sheet opened inside another stacks over it. For a task panel on the side, use Drawer. |
| `SheetCloseProps` | Type | Props of the web Sheet.Close: the close-button contract plus HeroUI v3's own close-trigger props. |
| `SheetProps` | Type | Props of the web Sheet: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own dialog props, passed through to the dialog. HeroUI's `aria-label` is the contract's `label`, and its physical `left` and `right` edges are the contract's `start` and `end`. |
| `SheetTitleProps` | Type | Props of the web Sheet.Title: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own heading props, passed through. |
| `Shimmer` | Function | A loading shimmer over text, or placeholder lines. |
| `ShimmerProps` | Type | Props of the web Shimmer. |
| `ShortcutCheatSheet` | Function | The searchable, grouped list of every registered shortcut. |
| `ShortcutCheatSheetProps` | Type | Props of the web cheat sheet. |
| `ShortcutHint` | Function | The key hint of a binding, or of an older caller's ready-made text. |
| `ShortcutHintProps` | Interface | The props of the key hint. |
| `ShortcutSettingsPanel` | Function | The person's shortcut settings. |
| `ShortcutSettingsPanelProps` | Type | Props of the web shortcut settings panel. |
| `SidePanel` | Constant | A side panel that is part of the page: opening it pushes the page over, expanding it gives it the whole row. Put the page in `SidePanel.Inset` and the panel's `Header`, `Body` and `Footer` (and a `Handle` to resize it) in `SidePanel.Panel`; add a `SidePanel.Launcher` to open it from a floating bar. |
| `SidePanelApi` | Interface | The shared state of one side panel. |
| `SidePanelBodyProps` | Type | Props of SidePanel.Body: a `div`'s props. |
| `SidePanelFooterProps` | Type | Props of SidePanel.Footer: a `div`'s props. |
| `SidePanelHandleProps` | Type | Props of SidePanel.Handle: a `div`'s props; the handle has no content of its own. |
| `SidePanelHeaderProps` | Type | Props of SidePanel.Header: a `div`'s props plus its slots and built-in buttons. |
| `SidePanelInsetProps` | Type | Props of SidePanel.Inset: a `div`'s props. |
| `SidePanelLauncherProps` | Type | Props of SidePanel.Launcher: a `div`'s props plus its title, input slot and actions. |
| `SidePanelPanelProps` | Type | Props of SidePanel.Panel: an `aside`'s props; its name is the SidePanel's `label`. |
| `SidePanelProps` | Type | Props of the web SidePanel: the shared contract plus the layout row's `div` props, passed through. |
| `Sidebar` | Constant | A side column of navigation. Draw it from `items`, or compose its parts as children; put it in a `SidebarProvider` with a `Sidebar.Inset` to drive it from a `Sidebar.Trigger` in the page header and to have it become a drawer on a phone. Collapse it with `isCollapsed` and `onCollapsedChange`, or leave it to `defaultCollapsed`. |
| `SidebarContentProps` | Type | Props of the web Sidebar.Content: the landmark's `label` plus a `nav`'s props; its children are groups and menus. |
| `SidebarFooterProps` | Type | Props of the web Sidebar.Footer: a `div`'s props; its children are usually the user menu. |
| `SidebarGroupProps` | Type | Props of the web Sidebar.Group: a labelled block of menus, optionally folding, plus a `div`'s own props. |
| `SidebarHeaderProps` | Type | Props of the web Sidebar.Header: a `div`'s props; its children are the brand or a workspace switcher. |
| `SidebarInsetProps` | Type | Props of the web Sidebar.Inset: a `div`'s props; its children are the page beside the sidebar (put the page's `main` inside). |
| `SidebarMenuItemProps` | Type | Props of the web Sidebar.MenuItem: one place in a menu, plus the `li`'s own props. |
| `SidebarMenuProps` | Type | Props of the web Sidebar.Menu: a `ul`'s props; its children are `Sidebar.MenuItem`s. |
| `SidebarMenuSkeletonProps` | Type | Props of the web Sidebar.MenuSkeleton: a placeholder row while the menu loads. |
| `SidebarProps` | Type | Props of the web Sidebar: the shared contract, the web state props (read only when no `SidebarProvider` is around it, which then owns the state), and the root `div`'s own props, passed through. |
| `SidebarProvider` | Function | Shares one sidebar's state with everything inside it. |
| `SidebarProviderProps` | Type | Props of the web SidebarProvider: the state props, the layout it wraps, and the wrapper `div`'s own props. |
| `SidebarRailProps` | Type | Props of the web Sidebar.Rail: a `div`'s props; the rail has no content of its own. |
| `SidebarSearchProps` | Type | Props of the web Sidebar.Search: a SearchField's props. Its text is the sidebar's filter (`useSidebar().query`) unless `value` and `onChange` take it over; the spoken name defaults to the `ui` catalogue's "Filter navigation". |
| `SidebarSeparatorProps` | Type | Props of the web Sidebar.Separator: an `hr`'s props. |
| `SidebarState` | Interface | The shared state of one sidebar. |
| `SidebarStateProps` | Interface | The state props of a web sidebar. |
| `SidebarTriggerProps` | Type | Props of the web Sidebar.Trigger: an IconButton whose `icon` and `label` default to the sidebar's own (expand, collapse, or open the phone drawer). |
| `SingleColumnLayout` | Function | A page of one column: its children stacked from top to bottom with a gap. |
| `SingleColumnLayoutProps` | Type | Props of the web SingleColumnLayout: the shared contract plus the `div`'s own props, passed through. |
| `Skeleton` | Function | A placeholder for content that is still loading; its shape and size are its `className`. It is an empty box, so assistive technology never reaches it: name the loading region with a `SkeletonGroup`'s `loadingLabel`. |
| `SkeletonGroup` | Function | A region of Skeletons that load together, with one shared state and one sweep. With a `loadingLabel` it names the region to screen readers while it loads. The status element is rendered empty and filled after mount, because most screen readers announce a live region only when its text changes, not when it is inserted already full. Ending the load empties the element silently: a completion announcement needs its own text, which the contract does not carry. |
| `SkeletonGroupProps` | Type | Props of the web SkeletonGroup: the shared contract plus the props of its root `div`, passed through. |
| `SkeletonProps` | Type | Props of the web Skeleton: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own skeleton props, passed through unchanged. |
| `SlideToConfirm` | Function | A thumb the person slides to the end to confirm a weighty action. |
| `SlideToConfirmProps` | Type | Props of the web SlideToConfirm: the shared contract plus the root's own props, passed through. |
| `Slider` | Function | One thumb on one track. |
| `SliderProps` | Type | Props of the web Slider. |
| `SlotIcon` | Function | Draws an icon slot: an `IconName` becomes an `Icon`, an element is returned unchanged. |
| `SlotIconProps` | Interface | Props of SlotIcon. |
| `SocialSignInButtons` | Function | "Continue with …" buttons, one per sign-in provider. |
| `SocialSignInButtonsProps` | Type | Props of the web SocialSignInButtons: the shared contract plus the root's own props, passed through. |
| `SortableList` | Function | Rows reordered by drag, keyboard or screen reader. |
| `SortableListProps` | Type | Props of the web SortableList: the shared contract plus React Aria's `GridList` props, passed through to the root. The list draws its own rows and owns the drag and drop, so children and the drag hooks are refused, `label` owns the accessible name, and the class is a plain string. |
| `SourceChip` | Function | One citation, as a pill. |
| `SourceChipProps` | Type | Props of the web SourceChip. |
| `Sources` | Function | An answer's citations, folded. |
| `SourcesProps` | Type | Props of the web Sources. |
| `Sparkline` | Function | A tiny trend. |
| `SparklineProps` | Type | Props of the web Sparkline. |
| `Spinner` | Function | The dot loader's indeterminate cell: one status when labelled, hidden otherwise. |
| `SpinnerProps` | Type | The shared contract plus the span's own props; the name and the hiding are the contract's `label`. |
| `SplitButton` | Function | A primary action with a caret that opens its related actions, for example "Save" with "Save and new". |
| `SplitButtonProps` | Type | Props of the web SplitButton: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own ButtonGroup props, passed through unchanged. The two halves are the component's, so `children`, `orientation` and `fullWidth` are refused. |
| `SplitView` | Function | A list and its detail, side by side when they fit and one at a time on a phone. |
| `SplitViewProps` | Type | Props of the web SplitView: the shared contract, the resize props, and the root's own props, passed through. With `isResizable`, `primaryWidth` is the starting width and the strip between the panes resizes the list pane. |
| `Stack` | Function | Blocks in a column with an even gap. |
| `StackProps` | Type | Props of the web Stack. |
| `StackedMeter` | Function | One bar of parts of a whole. |
| `StackedMeterProps` | Type | Props of the web StackedMeter. |
| `Stat` | Function | A labelled headline figure. |
| `StatProps` | Type | Props of the web Stat: the shared contract. |
| `StatusBadge` | Function | A record's state: its name as text, with a dot in the state's tone before it. The chip stays neutral so the text keeps its contrast; the dot only repeats the meaning in colour. |
| `StatusBadgeProps` | Type | Props of the web StatusBadge: the shared contract plus the Chip's pass-through props. |
| `StatusIndicator` | Function | A state as colour, shape and text together, pulsing when it needs attention. |
| `StatusIndicatorProps` | Type | Props of the web StatusIndicator: the shared contract plus the attributes of its `span`. |
| `Stepper` | Function | The steps of a multi-step task. |
| `StepperProps` | Type | Props of the web Stepper. |
| `SunburstChart` | Function | Nested shares as rings. |
| `SunburstChartProps` | Type | Props of the web SunburstChart. |
| `Surface` | Function | A background at one of the theme's surface levels. HeroUI keeps the level in `SurfaceContext`, so HeroUI fields inside pick colours that sit on it. The web surface paints colour only: add padding and radius with `className`. |
| `SurfaceProps` | Type | Props of the web Surface: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own surface props, passed through unchanged. |
| `SwipeableTabs` | Function | Tabs whose panels also change with a swipe. |
| `SwipeableTabsProps` | Type | Props of the web SwipeableTabs: the shared contract plus the root's own props, passed through. |
| `Switch` | Function | A toggle for a setting that takes effect at once. `label` names it; `description` and `errorMessage` sit under the control and are read out with it. The thumb moves toward the end of the reading direction when on, so it follows right-to-left on its own. |
| `SwitchProps` | Type | Props of the web Switch: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own switch props, passed through unchanged. HeroUI's `isSelected`, `defaultSelected`, string `value` and `children` are refused: the contract names each of them once. |
| `SystemBanner` | Function | An app-wide message that slides in from the top and collapses away when dismissed. |
| `SystemBannerProps` | Type | Props of the web SystemBanner: the shared contract plus the Alert's pass-through props. |
| `TabBar` | Function | A bottom bar of up to five tabs. |
| `TabBarProps` | Type | Props of the web TabBar. |
| `Table` | Function | A table of rows and columns. The rows are `items`, the cells come from `renderCell` or from the property each column's `id` names, and the selection and the sort are controlled by the app: `onSelectionChange` reports the ids now selected, and `onSortChange` the sort a press on a sortable header asks for. |
| `TableProps` | Type | Props of the web Table: the shared contract plus the `div` props of HeroUI's table root, passed through unchanged (a `className`, a `data-*` attribute, an `id`). The app lists rows as `items` and cells as `renderCell`, so children are refused. |
| `Tabs` | Function | A row of tabs over one panel each. The selection is the list of the one selected id; `onSelectionChange` is called with it whenever the user picks another tab. |
| `TabsProps` | Type | Props of the web Tabs: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own tabs props, passed through unchanged. The tabs are `items`, never children, and the selection is a list of ids, never HeroUI's `selectedKey`. |
| `TagGroup` | Function | A row of tags that can be selected and removed. The value is the list of selected keys; `onChange` is called with the new list, and `onRemove` with the keys of the tags the user removed, which the app then drops from `items`. |
| `TagGroupProps` | Type | Props of the web TagGroup: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own tag group props, passed through unchanged. The app lists tags as `items`, and the selection is the contract's `value`, never HeroUI's selected keys. |
| `TagInput` | Function | Free-text tags in one field. |
| `TagInputProps` | Type | Props of the web TagInput. |
| `TamoAskBox` | Function | Ask Tamo in words; review its proposal. |
| `TamoAskBoxProps` | Type | Props of the web TamoAskBox: the shared contract plus a class for its root. |
| `TamoButton` | Function | The Tamo action button. |
| `TamoButtonProps` | Type | Props of the web TamoButton: the shared contract plus React Aria's button props (a menu or popover trigger's). |
| `TamoCollectionTools` | Function | Tamo's tools on a collection. |
| `TamoCollectionToolsProps` | Interface | Props of the web TamoCollectionTools. |
| `TamoContext` | Constant | The Tamo slot of the tree, or `null` with no AI module. |
| `TamoDraftReview` | Function | Tamo's draft beside the text it would replace. |
| `TamoDraftReviewProps` | Type | Props of the web TamoDraftReview: the shared contract plus a class for its root. |
| `TamoGhostText` | Function | Tamo's suggestion under a field. |
| `TamoGhostTextProps` | Type | Props of the web TamoGhostText: the shared contract plus a class for its root. |
| `TamoLauncher` | Function | The Ask Tamo launcher. |
| `TamoLauncherProps` | Type | Props of the web TamoLauncher: the shared contract plus a class for its root. |
| `TamoMark` | Function | Draws Tamo's mark, whose eyes scan while Tamo works and go flat on an error. |
| `TamoMarkProps` | Type | Props of the web TamoMark: the shared contract plus a class for its root. |
| `TamoMarker` | Function | The AI-made marker. |
| `TamoMarkerProps` | Type | Props of the web TamoMarker: the shared contract plus a class for its root. |
| `TamoMenu` | Function | Tamo's action menu on a text. |
| `TamoMenuProps` | Type | Props of the web TamoMenu: the shared contract plus a class for its root. |
| `TamoPanel` | Function | The Ask Tamo panel. |
| `TamoPanelProps` | Type | Props of the web TamoPanel: the shared contract plus a class for the drawer's body. |
| `TamoReviewContext` | Constant | The field names awaiting the person's check. |
| `TamoStatusLine` | Function | Tamo's working and error line. |
| `TamoStatusLineProps` | Type | Props of the web TamoStatusLine: the shared contract plus a class for its root. |
| `TaskTray` | Function | The app's background tasks, docked in a corner. |
| `TaskTrayProps` | Type | Props of the web TaskTray. |
| `TerminalDemo` | Function | A terminal that plays a recorded session line by line. |
| `TerminalDemoProps` | Interface | Props of the web TerminalDemo. |
| `Text` | Function | Running text in the theme's type scale. A `lang` marks text in another language than the page and sets its direction; the theme's `:lang(ar)` rule then draws it in the Arabic style. Pass `elementType="span"` to draw a `span` inside a sentence. |
| `TextArea` | Function | A labelled multi-line text field. A field without a visible `label` needs an `aria-label`. Pair `value` with `onChange` to control it, or give `defaultValue` and let it keep its own text; `onChange` receives the new string. |
| `TextAreaField` | Function | A multi-line text field ready for a schema form's binding. |
| `TextAreaFieldProps` | Type | Props of the web TextAreaField: the TextArea's own. |
| `TextAreaProps` | Type | Props of the web TextArea: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own text field props, passed through unchanged. HeroUI's `fullWidth` is the contract's `isFullWidth`; `children` is refused because the field draws its own parts, and `type` because an area has no kind of text. |
| `TextField` | Function | A labelled single-line text field. A field without a visible `label` needs an `aria-label`. Pair `value` with `onChange` to control it, or give `defaultValue` and let it keep its own text; `onChange` receives the new string. |
| `TextFieldProps` | Type | Props of the web TextField: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own text field props, passed through unchanged. HeroUI's `fullWidth` is the contract's `isFullWidth`, and `children` is refused because the field draws its own parts. |
| `TextProps` | Type | Props of the web Text: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3 Typography's own props, passed through unchanged. HeroUI's `color` and `truncate` are the contract's `tone` and `isTruncated`, so the type refuses them. |
| `TextReveal` | Function | A headline whose words fade in one after another. |
| `TextRevealProps` | Type | Props of the web TextReveal: the shared contract. |

| `TimeClock` | Function | A time of day on a clock face. |
| `TimeClockProps` | Type | Props of the web TimeClock. |
| `TimeField` | Function | A labelled field over a time of day. `end` is drawn at the inline end of the group (the TimePicker's button). |
| `TimeFieldProps` | Type | Props of the web TimeField. |

| `TimelineChartProps` | Type | Props of the web TimelineChart. |
| `TimelineProps` | Type | Props of the web Timeline. |

| `TocRailProps` | Type | Props of the web TocRail. |

| `TooltipProps` | Type | Props of the web Tooltip: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own tooltip root props, passed through. |

| `TrackerStripProps` | Type | Props of the web TrackerStrip. |

| `TreemapChartProps` | Type | Props of the web TreemapChart. |
| `TrendChip` | Function | A change against the previous period. |
| `TrendChipProps` | Type | Props of the web TrendChip. |
| `Truncate` | Function | Clamped text that can open. |
| `TruncateProps` | Type | Props of the web Truncate. |

| `UploadItem` | Interface | One file in an UploadList. |
| `UploadList` | Function | The files being uploaded, each with its progress, retry and remove. |
| `UploadListProps` | Type | Props of the web UploadList: the shared contract plus the root `ul`'s own props, passed through. The wrapper draws the rows and `label` names the list, so `children` and `aria-label` are refused. |

| `ViewStackBackProps` | Type | Props of the web ViewStack.Back: the shared contract. |
| `ViewStackProps` | Type | Props of the web ViewStack: the shared contract. |
| `ViewStackViewProps` | Type | Props of the web ViewStack.View: the shared contract. |

| `VisuallyHiddenProps` | Type | Props of the web VisuallyHidden: the shared contract plus React Aria's own (`elementType`, `isFocusable`). |

| `VoiceInputProps` | Type | Props of the web VoiceInput. |
| `WaffleChart` | Function | Parts of a whole as squares. |
| `WaffleChartProps` | Type | Props of the web WaffleChart. |
| `WaterfallChart` | Function | A bridge from one total to the next. |

### `AlertDialog`

Function · declared in `src/react/alert-dialog/alert-dialog.tsx`

```ts
function AlertDialog({ title, description, children, confirmLabel, cancelLabel, tone = "default", icon, isOpen, defaultOpen = false, onOpenChange, isDismissable = false, isKeyboardDismissDisabled = false, isConfirmDisabled = false, onConfirm, onCancel, ...rest }: AlertDialogProps): ReactElement;
```

A modal that interrupts the user to confirm or refuse one decision. Confirming calls `onConfirm` and closes it;
every other way out (cancel, Escape, a press outside when `isDismissable`) calls `onCancel` and closes it. Pair
`isOpen` with `onOpenChange` to control it, or leave both out and use `defaultOpen`.

Type · declared in `src/react/alert-dialog/alert-dialog.props.ts`

```ts
type AlertDialogProps = AlertDialogContractProps & Omit<AlertDialogDialogProps, keyof AlertDialogContractProps | "children" | "role">;
```

Props of the web AlertDialog: the shared contract plus React Aria's dialog props, passed to the dialog element
(`className`, `id`, `aria-describedby`, `data-*`). HeroUI's `role` is fixed at `alertdialog`, and its children are
the contract's `title`, `description` and `children`, so the type refuses both. HeroUI's `status`, which is the
contract's `tone`, is not a dialog prop, so the type refuses that as well.

the role is the tone's, never the caller's.

| `readonly actions?: readonly { readonly id: string; readonly label: string; readonly href: string; readonly variant?: "primary" \| "ghost" \| undefined; }[] \| undefined;` | Up to two pill links under the text; `primary` is filled, `ghost` (the default) is quiet. |

| `readonly mediaBleed?: "none" \| "bottom" \| "full" \| undefined;` | How the media meets the card's edge: `bottom` (the default) runs it to the bottom edge, `full` to every edge. |

| `readonly brand?: Theme \| undefined;` | A product's theme: the card draws in that brand's tokens, as on the "Built on" strip. |
| `readonly span?: 1 \| 2 \| undefined;` | The columns the card spans in a two-column bento grid. Defaults to 1. |
| `readonly className?: string \| undefined;` | Styles the card. |

### `ButtonLink`

Function · declared in `src/react/button-link/button-link.tsx`

```ts
function ButtonLink({ variant = "primary", shape, size, className, children, ...rest }: ButtonLinkProps): ReactElement;
```

A link that looks like a button.

**Example**

```ts
<ButtonLink href="/docs" variant="core" shape="pill">Read the docs</ButtonLink>
```

### `ButtonLinkProps`

Interface · declared in `src/react/button-link/button-link.props.ts`

```ts
interface ButtonLinkProps extends Omit<AriaLinkProps, "children" | "className" | "style"> {
    readonly href: NonNullable<AriaLinkProps["href"]>;
    readonly children: ReactNode;
    readonly variant?: ButtonVariant | undefined;
    readonly shape?: ButtonShape | undefined;
    readonly size?: Size | undefined;
    readonly className?: string | undefined;
}
```

Props of the web ButtonLink: React Aria's link props (the `href` required) plus the Button's look: `variant`,
`shape` and `size`, with the Button's defaults.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly href: NonNullable<AriaLinkProps["href"]>;` | Where the link goes; an internal one navigates through the nearest `RouterProvider`. |
| `readonly children: ReactNode;` | The visible words, which are also the link's spoken name. |
| `readonly variant?: ButtonVariant \| undefined;` | The emphasis and colour, as the Button's. Defaults to `primary`. |
| `readonly shape?: ButtonShape \| undefined;` | The corner: the theme's own (`default`) or a full pill (`pill`). Defaults to `default`. |
| `readonly size?: Size \| undefined;` | The height, padding and text size, as the Button's. Defaults to `md`. |
| `readonly className?: string \| undefined;` | Styles the link. |

### `ButtonProps`

Type · declared in `src/react/button/button.props.ts`

```ts
type ButtonProps = ButtonContractProps & Omit<HeroButtonProps, keyof ButtonContractProps | "isPending" | "fullWidth" | "isIconOnly" | "aria-label" | "href" | "target"> & {
    readonly href?: string | undefined;
    readonly target?: HTMLAttributeAnchorTarget | undefined;
};
```

Props of the web Button: the shared contract plus HeroUI v3's own button props, passed through unchanged. HeroUI's
`isPending`, `fullWidth`, `isIconOnly` and `aria-label` are the contract's `isLoading`, `isFullWidth`,
`IconButton` and `label`, so the type refuses them: one concept, one prop. `href` and `target` make it a link
drawn as the button.

### `Component`

Class · declared in `src/react/component/component.ts`

```ts
abstract class Component<P extends object = object> {
    readonly props: Readonly<P>;
    readonly state: ReactComponent<P>["state"];
    readonly context: ReactComponent<P>["context"];
    readonly setState: ReactComponent<P>["setState"];
    readonly forceUpdate: ReactComponent<P>["forceUpdate"];
    abstract render(): ReactNode;
}
```

The base of a web class component.

**Example**

```ts
export class Greeting extends Component<GreetingProps> {
  render() {
    return <Text>{this.props.name}</Text>;
  }
}
```

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly props: Readonly<P>;` | The props the host passed; a component never writes them. |
| `readonly state: ReactComponent<P>["state"];` | React's state slot; a class component's fields are its state once compiled. |
| `readonly context: ReactComponent<P>["context"];` | React's context slot; a component reads a context through a hook field. |
| `readonly setState: ReactComponent<P>["setState"];` | React's setter; a component writes a field instead. |
| `readonly forceUpdate: ReactComponent<P>["forceUpdate"];` | React's re-render; never needed, since a field write re-renders. |
| `abstract render(): ReactNode;` | What the component draws. |

### `DataTableRows`

Interface · declared in `src/react/data-table/data-table-rows.type.ts`

```ts
interface DataTableRows<TItem extends CollectionItem = CollectionItem> {
    readonly onReorder?: ((items: readonly TItem[]) => void) | undefined;
    readonly hasHoverActions?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly hasContextMenu?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly renderRowDetail?: ((item: TItem) => ReactNode) | undefined;
    readonly expandedKeys?: readonly string[] | undefined;
    readonly defaultExpandedKeys?: readonly string[] | undefined;
    readonly onExpandedChange?: ((keys: readonly string[]) => void) | undefined;
    readonly onRowOpen?: ((item: TItem) => void) | undefined;
    readonly renderRowPanel?: ((item: TItem) => ReactNode) | undefined;
    readonly onCellEdit?: ((item: TItem, columnId: string, value: DataGridValue) => void | Promise<void>) | undefined;
}
```

The web DataTable's row tools.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly onReorder?: ((items: readonly TItem[]) => void) \| undefined;` | Given, each row gets a drag handle (pointer, keyboard and screen reader, through React Aria's drag and drop) and a drop calls this with the rows shown in their new order (group and total rows left out). Nothing moves until the app passes the rows back; reordering a sorted view is the app's call. |
| `readonly hasHoverActions?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to draw the row actions that have an icon as buttons revealed on hover or keyboard focus, beside the row menu, which still lists them all. |
| `readonly hasContextMenu?: boolean \| undefined;` | False to leave the browser's own menu on a right-click; by default a row's right-click (or Shift+F10) opens its actions. |
| `readonly renderRowDetail?: ((item: TItem) => ReactNode) \| undefined;` | Given, each row gets a disclosure button and an expanded row shows this under it across every column. |
| `readonly expandedKeys?: readonly string[] \| undefined;` | The ids of the expanded rows, for a controlled expansion. |
| `readonly defaultExpandedKeys?: readonly string[] \| undefined;` | The ids of the rows expanded at first. |
| `readonly onExpandedChange?: ((keys: readonly string[]) => void) \| undefined;` | Called with the ids of the expanded rows after a row or "expand all" is pressed. |
| `readonly onRowOpen?: ((item: TItem) => void) \| undefined;` | Called when a row is opened: a press on the row (Enter from the keyboard). |
| `readonly renderRowPanel?: ((item: TItem) => ReactNode) \| undefined;` | Given, an opened row shows this in a resizable side panel beside the table (a drawer on a small screen). |
| `readonly onCellEdit?: ((item: TItem, columnId: string, value: DataGridValue) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined;` | Called with a valid edited value of a column marked `isEditable` (its `type` picks the DataGrid editor, its rules validate the value); the app writes the row. A returned promise is awaited: a rejection is shown on the cell. |

### `DataViewScale`

Interface · declared in `src/react/data-view/data-view-scale.type.ts`

```ts

}
```

The web DataView's scale tools.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly isVirtualized?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to draw only the rows, cards or board cards in view (React Aria's Virtualizer), for 10,000 rows and more. |
| `readonly cardsBelow?: number \| null \| undefined;` | The DataView's own width, in CSS pixels, under which the table view is drawn as cards (the engine's layout stays `table`, so a wider frame shows the table again). Defaults to 600; `null` keeps the table at any width. |
| `readonly board?: DataViewBoard<TItem> \| undefined;` | Given, the view switcher offers the Board: cards in columns by one field, moved by drag or from a menu. |

### `DitherArt`

Function · declared in `src/react/dither-art/dither-art.tsx`

```ts
function DitherArt({ pattern = "strata", front = "accent", back = "background", core = "core", pixelSize = 4, mode = "dots", glyphs = GLYPHS, sun, speed = 1, poster, mask = "none", className, }: DitherArtProps): ReactElement;
```

A brand pattern, dithered on a canvas over its poster.

### `DitherArtProps`

Interface · declared in `src/react/dither-art/dither-art.props.ts`

```ts
interface DitherArtProps {
    readonly pattern?: DitherPattern | undefined;
    readonly front?: string | undefined;
    readonly back?: string | undefined;
    readonly core?: string | undefined;
    readonly pixelSize?: number | undefined;
    readonly mode?: "dots" | "glyphs" | undefined;
    readonly glyphs?: string | undefined;
    readonly sun?: DitherSun | undefined;
    readonly speed?: number | undefined;
    readonly poster: string | {
        readonly light: string;
        readonly dark: string;
    };
    readonly mask?: "none" | "radial" | "bottom" | "top" | undefined;
    readonly className?: string | undefined;
}
```

Props of the web DitherArt.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly pattern?: DitherPattern \| undefined;` | The brand pattern. Defaults to `strata`, the primary. |
| `readonly front?: string \| undefined;` | The steel dots' colour, a theme token name read as `--color-<name>`. Defaults to `accent`. |
| `readonly back?: string \| undefined;` | The ground's colour, a theme token name. Defaults to `background`. |
| `readonly core?: string \| undefined;` | The engine glow's colour (the champagne core), a theme token name. Defaults to `core`. |
| `readonly pixelSize?: number \| undefined;` | One dither cell in CSS pixels: 2 to 8 for `dots`, 2 to 24 for `glyphs`. Defaults to 4. |
| `readonly mode?: "dots" \| "glyphs" \| undefined;` | How a lit cell is drawn: `dots`, a square of its colour, or `glyphs`, a code character in the mono font at the cell size. Defaults to `dots`. |
| `readonly glyphs?: string \| undefined;` | The characters `glyphs` draws from. Defaults to `{}[]()<>/=+*#;`. |
| `readonly sun?: DitherSun \| undefined;` | Where the core glow (the sun) sits, as fractions of the art's width from the inline start and of its height, so in right-to-left it follows the mirrored layout. Defaults to the pattern's own. |
| `readonly speed?: number \| undefined;` | How fast the art drifts on real time; 0 is still. Defaults to 1, and is 0 whenever motion is reduced. |
| `readonly poster: string \| { readonly light: string; readonly dark: string; };` | The kit's poster image for this pattern, one for both schemes or one per scheme (`{ light, dark }`; dark first): what the edge render, a page without script and print show, under the canvas until it draws. |
| `readonly mask?: "none" \| "radial" \| "bottom" \| "top" \| undefined;` | A fade over the art: `radial` toward the edges, `bottom` or `top` toward that side. Defaults to `none`. |
| `readonly className?: string \| undefined;` | Styles the wrapper; give it a size, since the art fills it. |

### `List`

Function · declared in `src/react/list/list.tsx`

```ts
function List<TItem extends ListItemData = ListItemData>({ items, emptyState, isLoading = false, loadingLabel, label, variant, selectionMode = "none", selectedKeys, defaultSelectedKeys, onSelectionChange, hasMore, onLoadMore, isAnimated = true, isVirtualized = false, maxHeight = "32rem", style, ...rest }: ListProps<TItem>): ReactElement;
```

A list of rows. Name it with `label`. Give it `selectionMode` to make it a list box whose rows are selected by

### `PdfPreview`

Function · declared in `src/react/pdf-preview/pdf-preview.tsx`

```ts
function PdfPreview({ src, name, onDownload, className }: PdfPreviewProps): ReactElement;
```

A PDF in the browser's viewer, with a download fallback.

**Example**

```ts
<PdfPreview src={link.url} name="Weekly sales.pdf" onDownload={download} />
```

### `PdfPreviewProps`

Interface · declared in `src/react/pdf-preview/pdf-preview.props.ts`

```ts
interface PdfPreviewProps {
    readonly src: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly onDownload?: (() => void) | undefined;
    readonly className?: string | undefined;
}
```

Props of `PdfPreview`.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly src: string;` | The PDF's link: a short-lived signed URL minted for this open, on another origin than the page (object storage). A same-origin link is shown with scripts off, so the frame can never lift its own sandbox. |
| `readonly name: string;` | The file name, shown as given; it names the frame and the download button. |
| `readonly onDownload?: (() => void) \| undefined;` | Called by the download button, the fallback where the browser shows no PDF; left out, there is none. |
| `readonly className?: string \| undefined;` | Styles the root. |

### `SidePanel`

Constant · declared in `src/react/side-panel/side-panel.tsx`

```ts
const SidePanel: typeof SidePanelRoot & SidePanelParts;
```

A side panel that is part of the page: opening it pushes the page over, expanding it gives it the whole row. Put the
page in `SidePanel.Inset` and the panel's `Header`, `Body` and `Footer` (and a `Handle` to resize it) in
`SidePanel.Panel`; add a `SidePanel.Launcher` to open it from a floating bar.

### `SidePanelApi`

Interface · declared in `src/react/side-panel/side-panel-api.type.ts`

```ts
interface SidePanelApi {
    readonly panelId: string;
    readonly label: string;
    readonly state: SidePanelState;
    readonly setState: (state: SidePanelState) => void;
    readonly width: number;
    readonly setWidth: (width: number) => void;
    readonly minWidth: number;
    readonly maxWidth: number;
    readonly isDrawer: boolean;
    readonly isInitial: boolean;
    readonly variant: SidePanelVariant;
}
```

The shared state of one side panel.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly panelId: string;` | The id of the panel, for a control's `aria-controls`. |
| `readonly label: string;` | The panel's spoken name. |
| `readonly state: SidePanelState;` | The state now. |
| `readonly setState: (state: SidePanelState) => void;` | Moves to a state. |
| `readonly width: number;` | The open width in pixels, within the bounds. |
| `readonly setWidth: (width: number) => void;` | Sets the open width; the value is clamped to the bounds. |
| `readonly minWidth: number;` | The narrowest width. |
| `readonly maxWidth: number;` | The widest width. |
| `readonly isDrawer: boolean;` | True below `drawerBelow`, where the panel is a drawer over the page instead of a column of it. |
| `readonly isInitial: boolean;` | True until the state first changes after mount; a drawer opens only from a change, never on load. |
| `readonly variant: SidePanelVariant;` | The look: a bordered column, or a card split from the page. |

### `SidebarState`

Interface · declared in `src/react/sidebar/sidebar-state.type.ts`

```ts
interface SidebarState {
    readonly panelId: string;
    readonly isCollapsed: boolean;
    readonly setCollapsed: (isCollapsed: boolean) => void;
    readonly toggle: () => void;
    readonly collapsible: SidebarCollapsible;
    readonly variant: SidebarVariant;
    readonly isMobile: boolean;
    readonly isMobileOpen: boolean;
    readonly setMobileOpen: (isOpen: boolean) => void;
    readonly isRail: boolean;
    readonly isPeeking: boolean;
    readonly setPeeking: (isPeeking: boolean) => void;
    readonly isExpandOnHover: boolean;
    readonly width: number;
    readonly setWidth: (width: number) => void;
    readonly minWidth: number;
    readonly maxWidth: number;
    readonly isResizable: boolean;
    readonly query: string;
    readonly setQuery: (query: string) => void;
    readonly isProvided: boolean;
}
```

The shared state of one sidebar.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly panelId: string;` | The id of the sidebar's panel, for a trigger's `aria-controls`. |
| `readonly isCollapsed: boolean;` | True when the sidebar is collapsed (the rail, or slid away). |
| `readonly setCollapsed: (isCollapsed: boolean) => void;` | Collapses (true) or expands (false) the sidebar. |
| `readonly toggle: () => void;` | Flips the state the sidebar is in now: the phone drawer below the breakpoint, the collapsed state above it. |
| `readonly collapsible: SidebarCollapsible;` | How the sidebar collapses. |
| `readonly variant: SidebarVariant;` | The sidebar's look. |
| `readonly isMobile: boolean;` | True below the mobile breakpoint, where the sidebar is a drawer. |
| `readonly isMobileOpen: boolean;` | Whether the phone drawer is open. |
| `readonly setMobileOpen: (isOpen: boolean) => void;` | Opens or closes the phone drawer. |
| `readonly isRail: boolean;` | True while the parts draw as the icon rail: collapsed in `icon` mode, above the breakpoint, and not peeking. |
| `readonly isPeeking: boolean;` | True while a collapsed rail shows in full because the pointer rests on it (`isExpandOnHover`). |
| `readonly setPeeking: (isPeeking: boolean) => void;` | Starts or ends the peek. |
| `readonly isExpandOnHover: boolean;` | True when the rail opens in full while the pointer rests on it. |
| `readonly width: number;` | The expanded width in pixels. |
| `readonly setWidth: (width: number) => void;` | Sets the expanded width, clamped between `minWidth` and `maxWidth`. |
| `readonly minWidth: number;` | The narrowest the sidebar can be dragged, in pixels. |
| `readonly maxWidth: number;` | The widest the sidebar can be dragged, in pixels. |
| `readonly isResizable: boolean;` | True when the sidebar can be resized from its rail. |
| `readonly query: string;` | The filter text of `Sidebar.Search`. |
| `readonly setQuery: (query: string) => void;` | Sets the filter text. |
| `readonly isProvided: boolean;` | True when the state comes from an explicit `SidebarProvider`, false when a Sidebar made its own. |

### `SidebarStateProps`

Interface · declared in `src/react/sidebar/sidebar-state-props.type.ts`

```ts
interface SidebarStateProps extends Pick<SidebarContractProps, "variant" | "collapsible" | "isCollapsed" | "defaultCollapsed" | "onCollapsedChange" | "isMobileOpen" | "onMobileOpenChange"> {
    readonly defaultMobileOpen?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly collapseBelow?: LayoutBreakpoint | false | undefined;
    readonly keyboardShortcut?: string | false | undefined;
    readonly isExpandOnHover?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly isResizable?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly width?: number | undefined;
    readonly defaultWidth?: number | undefined;
    readonly minWidth?: number | undefined;
    readonly maxWidth?: number | undefined;
    readonly onWidthChange?: ((width: number) => void) | undefined;
    readonly storageKey?: string | undefined;
}
```

The state props of a web sidebar.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly defaultMobileOpen?: boolean \| undefined;` | The starting state of the phone drawer, for an uncontrolled drawer. Defaults to false. |
| `readonly collapseBelow?: LayoutBreakpoint \| false \| undefined;` | Below this breakpoint the sidebar is a drawer opened by `Sidebar.Trigger`; false keeps it in the page at every width. Defaults to `md` under a `SidebarProvider` and to false for a Sidebar drawn on its own. |
| `readonly keyboardShortcut?: string \| false \| undefined;` | The key that toggles the sidebar with Cmd (macOS) or Ctrl, or false for none. Defaults to `b`. |
| `readonly isExpandOnHover?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to show a collapsed rail in full while the pointer rests on it, over the page. Defaults to false. |
| `readonly isResizable?: boolean \| undefined;` | True to let the user drag (or arrow-key) the rail to set the width. Defaults to false. |
| `readonly width?: number \| undefined;` | The expanded width in pixels, for a controlled width; pair it with `onWidthChange`. |
| `readonly defaultWidth?: number \| undefined;` | The starting expanded width in pixels. Defaults to 256 (16 rem). |
| `readonly minWidth?: number \| undefined;` | The narrowest width a drag reaches, in pixels. Defaults to 208. |
| `readonly maxWidth?: number \| undefined;` | The widest width a drag reaches, in pixels. Defaults to 400. |
| `readonly onWidthChange?: ((width: number) => void) \| undefined;` | Called with the new width in pixels after each resize. |
| `readonly storageKey?: string \| undefined;` | A key under which the collapsed state and the width are kept in the browser's local storage, so they survive a reload. Left out, nothing is stored. |

Function · declared in `src/react/skeleton/skeleton.tsx`

```ts
function Skeleton({ isLoading, animationType, children, ...rest }: SkeletonProps): ReactNode;
```

A placeholder for content that is still loading; its shape and size are its `className`. It is an empty box, so
assistive technology never reaches it: name the loading region with a `SkeletonGroup`'s `loadingLabel`.

### `SkeletonGroup`

Function · declared in `src/react/skeleton-group/skeleton-group.tsx`

```ts
function SkeletonGroup({ isLoading = true, animationType = "shimmer", loadingLabel, className, children, ...rest }: SkeletonGroupProps): ReactElement;
```

A region of Skeletons that load together, with one shared state and one sweep. With a `loadingLabel` it names the
region to screen readers while it loads. The status element is rendered empty and filled after mount, because most
screen readers announce a live region only when its text changes, not when it is inserted already full. Ending the
load empties the element silently: a completion announcement needs its own text, which the contract does not carry.

Props of the web TerminalDemo.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly command: string;` | The command in the top bar, which the copy button copies, such as `stackra create my-app`. |
| `readonly label?: string \| undefined;` | The mono label in the top bar, and the region's spoken name. Defaults to "Try it". |
| `readonly lines: readonly TerminalLine[];` | The recorded session, played a whole line at a time. |
| `readonly result?: ReactNode;` | What the session ends on, such as a framed localhost preview; shown once every line is. |
| `readonly autoplay?: "in-view" \| "always" \| "never" \| undefined;` | When the session plays: once it scrolls into view (the default), at once, or `never` (the last frame only). |
| `readonly stepMs?: number \| undefined;` | The time between lines in milliseconds, by default the theme's `floor` motion step. |
| `readonly isCopyable?: boolean \| undefined;` | Shows the copy button. Defaults to true. |
| `readonly copyLabel?: string \| undefined;` | The copy button's spoken name. Defaults to "Copy command". |
| `readonly className?: string \| undefined;` | Styles the window. |

### `UseCollectionOptions`

Interface · declared in `src/react/hooks/use-collection-options.type.ts`

```ts
interface UseCollectionOptions<TItem extends CollectionItem = CollectionItem> {
    readonly columns: readonly CollectionColumn[];
    readonly items?: readonly TItem[] | undefined;
    readonly mode?: CollectionMode | undefined;
    readonly paging?: CollectionPaging | undefined;
    readonly initialState?: Partial<CollectionState> | undefined;
    readonly state?: Partial<CollectionState> | undefined;
    readonly onStateChange?: ((state: CollectionState) => void) | undefined;
    readonly collectionKey?: string | undefined;
    readonly store?: CollectionStore | null | undefined;
    readonly views?: readonly CollectionView[] | undefined;
    readonly defaultViewId?: string | null | undefined;
    readonly source?: CollectionSource<TItem> | undefined;
    readonly fetch?: ((query: CollectionQuery, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<CollectionPage<TItem>>) | undefined;
    readonly totalCount?: number | undefined;
    readonly isLoading?: boolean | undefined;
    readonly nextCursor?: string | null | undefined;
    readonly locale?: string | undefined;
}
```

The options of `useCollection`.

| Member | Description |
| ------ | ----------- |
| `readonly columns: readonly CollectionColumn[];` | The columns, in their defined order. |
| `readonly items?: readonly TItem[] \| undefined;` | Client mode: every row. Server mode without `fetch`: the rows of the page the app fetched for `query`. |
| `readonly mode?: CollectionMode \| undefined;` | `client` (the default) or `server`. |
| `readonly paging?: CollectionPaging \| undefined;` | How the rows are paged. Defaults to `none`. |
| `readonly initialState?: Partial<CollectionState> \| undefined;` | The state to start from, over the defaults; a saved state or default view wins over it. |
| `readonly state?: Partial<CollectionState> \| undefined;` | The controlled slices of the state. |
| `readonly onStateChange?: ((state: CollectionState) => void) \| undefined;` | Called with the whole next state on every change. |
| `readonly collectionKey?: string \| undefined;` | The key the state and views persist under; left out, nothing persists. |
| `readonly store?: CollectionStore \| null \| undefined;` | Where they persist. Defaults to the browser's `localStorage` when `collectionKey` is given; `null` for nowhere. |
| `readonly views?: readonly CollectionView[] \| undefined;` | The saved views to start with, when the store has none. |
| `readonly defaultViewId?: string \| null \| undefined;` | The view applied on open, when the store has none. |
| `readonly source?: CollectionSource<TItem> \| undefined;` | Server mode: the server adapter whose `fetch` answers each query (`fetch` below wins when both are given). |
| `readonly fetch?: ((query: CollectionQuery, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<CollectionPage<TItem>>) \| undefined;` | Server mode: fetches the page of a query; the engine aborts a fetch a newer query replaces. |
| `readonly totalCount?: number \| undefined;` | Server mode without `fetch`: how many rows match in all. Defaults to the rows given. |
| `readonly isLoading?: boolean \| undefined;` | Server mode without `fetch`: true while the app fetches. |
| `readonly nextCursor?: string \| null \| undefined;` | Server mode without `fetch`, `cursor` paging: the cursor of the page after the rows given, or `null` when they are the last; `loadMore` asks for it and Previous goes back through the cursors shown. |
| `readonly locale?: string \| undefined;` | The locale text is sorted in. Defaults to `en`. |
